# B1 explicit-copy regeneration: the listed sites (2026-10-22)

The output of `yo fix ./src --migrate explicit-copy --hot-path src/evaluator --std-path ./std`, by the `fix/explicit-copy-migration-defects` tree binary (3cb59050d), on that branch's tree. It holds the 1,956 sites the run left as written, followed by its summary line. Positions are the pre-rewrite tree's. The 646 inserted `.clone()` calls are the first commit of `feat/v2b-b1-regeneration`. The run, its measurements and the NO-GO verdict are in `plans/VALUES_BY_DEFAULT.md`, B1, "As-built 2026-10-22". This listing is the input for B3 and B4 once a regeneration is GO; the sites move when the tree does, so re-run the dry run on the tip before using it.
